## PROJECT EVIDENCE — editions/monroe-1.3/book-03-no-path-given/packets/MOVEMENT-007.md

# Movement brief — MOVEMENT-007 · "The Older Word"

- PWA book id: the-fractured-path / book-03-no-path-given (monroe-1.3 edition)
- Series id: the-fractured-path
- Author: Opus (Claude Opus 5.5), O'Connor seat 1.3.0
- Chapters: 47–53 (seven chapters) · Word budget: ~35,000 · Week 21
- Source chapters to read first, in full: 18, 19, 20
- Canon to load: `BOOK_MAP.md` (§7 Havel and Karis; §9 P12, P16; §10; §12 items 3, 9, 10, 17), `STATE_LEDGER.md` (as appended), Book 4 ch18 lines 87 and 230–237, Book 6 ch16 lines 65–75, Book 7 ch15 lines 80–100 (read for the boundaries the older word must not cross)
- Owner names: canon.

## Where we enter

Week 21. The outline holds. Coss holds the D6 record. Karis's days are in the founding-era shelves; her evenings are in the schedule beside Cael.

## What this movement is for

Deliberately the quietest movement: a documents war. The adversary's shape fixed; a buried word found, copied and kept unused; and the night before, when each person who loves him gives him one true thing. Physical anchors: the deposition fought as a duel of answers, Prynn's clause-by-clause defense, Lira's dusk spar.

Consequential turns:
- The delegation a week ahead: Yorlan (procedure embodied; two questions); Ilsev (from B1's senior evaluation; famous in the digests for ruling against her own side when its paper fails); Havel, promoted one grade, recording officer. "Institutions re-use people."
- Quenna's deposition rules: empty hands; you can't win a deposition, only lose one; "Answer the question asked. Never the question implied."
- **The deposition** (~2,500): cooperative, minimal, nothing volunteered; "That's a classification question."; "I understand that everything may be noted. That's what a record is."; the one question built for the hour answered truthfully.
- **Havel cutaway (~5,000 across arrival, the deposition from the recording desk, and the afternoon):** Ilsev's review in sequence; the designation in the routing history of the months-old request; stated authority two grades too low; predates the file; the origin query and its return; "administrative irregularity, referred" (P16 phrasing); she reads it a third time; his third private entry, dated, dry, shown to nobody.
- Coss in the yard: "Three weeks in the archive. You're going to argue the code." — "You're guessing." — "I'm reading." He won't requisition Prynn's lending ledger; he hopes it's a good argument. "Five days."
- The inventory notice; **Prynn vs the delegation's records officer, clause by clause** (~1,200 on the page), lost on the single word *unclassified*; "Their code forgot to write the word that gives them you. It remembered to write the word that takes my shelves."
- **Karis cutaway (~3,000):** her card system; clerks she has named by their handwriting; the waystation's own founding entry, read as a date (about four hundred years by that ledger) — a fact, not a meaning. Ends before the find.
- **Karis's independent read of the schedule** with Cael in the evenings: a second source for the absence ("you're the second source now; act like one" belongs to the copying, but the principle starts here).
- The older word (Cael POV): the crosshatched-sevens clerk; the entry and the later hand's annotation (P12 verbatim); his own older copy beside it; "Shattered is an editorial choice… The question is whose." — keep the question, drop any date on the rename. Both copies verified aloud. The clerks box the run two mornings later; Prynn demands a signed inventory line per box and writes one dated line in her restoration ledger.
- Cael declines to use it: the hearing must be won inside their text; producing an older custom concedes that custom can carry legal weight. "Into the notebook."
- The rehearsal: Karis as magistrate from a bench of books, rebuilding Yorlan's formalities from Prynn's digests of his earlier hearings; Brom as Coss, unnervingly; "a hole in the world" and the answer ("The remedy for a hole in the law is to write the law."); the custom objection and its counter, drilled until he can say "Custom is not code" asleep.
- The eve gifts: Lira's dusk spar ("This is real. Start from that."; the records line returned to him); Brom's page (internal protected line); Karis's conclusion page ("Findings to date: the phenomenon is a person. Study continues."); Prynn: "Shelf them yourself. You know where they live now." Log: "Tomorrow I argue that their system never contained me…" (internal protected).

## What must remain true / withheld

- No narration that the waystation predates Arbiters or the registry. The entry is founding-era by the archive's own ledger; the annotation is "a later hand." No number on the rename.
- Nobody, including Karis, theorizes what UNBOUND means or who ordered the change.
- Ilsev's query returns nothing; Havel tells nobody; Cael learns none of it.
- Quenna's "second sitting… may not be interrupted" note does NOT appear on this night (it moves to M8's night between sittings).
- The designation's name is "Suppression-Advisory Watch, Priority Level 4."

## What the author owns

The deposition's questions beyond the skeleton; Prynn's clauses; Karis's cards and named clerks; the rehearsal's jokes; the order of the gifts; whether Havel's cutaway is one block or two. Progression density ~35 per 10k (do not count the legal register). Scene breaks `---` about every 950 words.

## Where we leave pressure

The night before the first sitting. Nothing left to fix in the argument. A page in his pocket, a conclusion in a notebook, a yard that took the weight off. Morning brings a room that has come to watch him lose.
